=== add PANEL-TABS-SPEC.md
new 93f8e181a509539ff1763acf
+# Tabs in the agent's browser (the right panel)
+
+The developer, 2026-11-14: "I think we should add tabs to the iframe browser. If there's only 1 page open, we don't
+show a tab bar, if there is we do. The tabs should have titles (to display) and labels or ids that can be passed to say
+which tab the agent is talking about. The plugin should be able to list tabs and every tab must have a label/id."
+
+Three parts agree on this contract: code_boss's page and server (`src/ui.html`, `src/server.mjs`), the Node broker
+(`src/agent/panel-broker.mjs`, outside the container) and the Java broker (`sandbox-launcher/.../PanelBroker.java`, in
+the container's manager). The brokers own the tabs; the server keeps the list the page draws; the page draws it.
+
+## A tab
+
+| field   | what |
+|---------|------|
+| `tab`   | its id: 32 hex characters the broker makes. It IS the tab's DevTools target id (what `Target.getTargets` lists, what `attachToTarget` takes) and the `id` host.browser answers with. The one exception is the tab an old broker opens (below): `default`. |
+| `label` | the name a plugin gave it (`host.browser.open({ label })`), or `tab-<n>` for one opened over DevTools (`Target.createTarget`, `/json/new`). Unique among the open tabs. 1-40 letters, digits, `. _ -`. |
+| `title` | the page's title, for the tab bar (empty until the page has one) |
+| `url`   | the page's address |
+
+## Broker -> server: `POST /api/panel-browser` (what the page is told)
+
+| body | meaning |
+|------|---------|
+| `{ tab, label, url, show }` | put `url` in that tab - a NEW tab when `tab` is not open yet (with `label`). `show: true` brings the panel and that tab to the front. |
+| `{ tab, url, at: true, title? }` | the tab's page IS at `url` already (the agent or the developer navigated it; the broker saw it): recorded, shown, never loaded again. `title` updates the tab's title. |
+| `{ tab, title }` | the tab's title changed |
+| `{ tab, show: true }` | bring that tab to the front (no url: nothing is loaded) |
+| `{ tab, close: true }` | the tab is closed: its iframe goes |
+| no `tab` | an OLD broker (one page): the tab `default`, whose iframe is `#agentFrame`. Old brokers keep working. |
+
+The answer: `{ ok, tab, url }`, or 400 `{ error }`.
+
+## Server -> page
+
+`session.panelTabs = [{ tab, label, url, title }]` (in the order they were opened) and `session.panelActiveTab` - in the
+state payload and in every `panel-browser` broadcast (`{ type: 'panel-browser', tabs, active, tab, url, show, at }`).
+`session.panelBrowserUrl` stays: the active tab's url. A broker stopped (the setting off) leaves no tabs.
+
+## The page (`ui.html`)
+
+- One iframe per tab in `.rpb-frame-wrap`: `<iframe class="rpb-frame" data-cb-tab="<tab>">`. The tab `default` uses the
+  iframe that is always there, `#agentFrame` (`data-cb-tab="default"`). A broker finds a tab's iframe with
+  `document.querySelector('iframe[data-cb-tab="<tab>"]')` (for `default`: `#agentFrame`, as before).
+- Only the active tab's iframe is shown; the others stay laid out at their size, hidden (`visibility: hidden`) - like a
+  tab behind another in Chrome, they may not paint: a broker brings a tab to the front (`show`) before input to it.
+- The tab bar shows only with TWO OR MORE tabs: each tab's title (its label when the page has none), the active one
+  marked; a click shows that tab (and tells the server: `{ tab, show: true }`). The developer does not close tabs here:
+  opening and closing them is the agent's (the developer, 2026-11-14: "leave them fully controlled by ai").
+- The address line, reload and typing an address act on the active tab.
+- `panelBrowserSize(w, h, tab)` sizes that tab's page (no tab: `default`).
+
+## The brokers
+
+- `open({ url, label })`: a tab with that label (default `default`) - reused when it is open (navigated to `url` unless
+  it is about:blank, and brought to the front), else a new tab. Answers that tab's brief.
+- A tab's brief: `{ ok, id: <tab>, targetId: <tab>, tab: <tab>, label, title, url, active, cdpUrl, wsUrl, pageWsUrl, downloads,
+  panel: true, reused, note }` (+ the Java's `pid`, `insidePort`, `bridged`...). `wsUrl` is the browser's address, the
+  same for every tab; `pageWsUrl` is `ws://.../devtools/page/<tab>`.
+- `list()`: every open tab's brief. `close(id)`: the tab with that id or label; `panel` (or the Java's `panel-<n>`)
+  closes every tab. Closing the last tab leaves the panel empty; the next `open()` makes a tab again.
+- DevTools, browser level: `Target.getTargets` lists every tab; `createTarget({ url })` opens a new tab (label
+  `tab-<n>`), brings it to the front and answers its target id; `attachToTarget({ targetId })` gives a session on that
+  tab (one per tab per connection); `closeTarget` closes that tab; `activateTarget` brings it to the front;
+  `setDiscoverTargets` sends `targetCreated` for each tab and then `targetCreated` / `targetInfoChanged` /
+  `targetDestroyed` as tabs open, change and close; browser-level `setAutoAttach` attaches every tab, and each new one
+  as it opens. `/json/list` lists every tab, `/json/new?<url>` opens one, `/json/activate/<tab>`, `/json/close/<tab>`;
+  `/devtools/page/<tab>` is that tab's own page connection.
+- On a tab's session everything is as on the one page before (DENY, Page.navigate's site, dialogs, framing, frames
+  inside the page, sticky calls replayed on re-attach), per tab. `Page.bringToFront` shows that tab, `Page.close`
+  closes it, `Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride` sizes it (`panelBrowserSize(w, h, tab)`).
+- A dialog announced on the window's session goes to the tab whose page it came from (its url), else the active tab.
+- Developer navigations inside a tab are told back with `{ tab, url, at: true }`; title changes with `{ tab, title }`.
+- Each broker keeps ONE Chrome connection of its own while it has tabs (the watcher): it reads every tab's title (on
+  attach, DOMContentLoaded, load, in-page navigation, and every second - a title the page's own script sets later,
+  with no load, reaches the tab bar within about a second; one read per tab at a time) and reports the developer's
+  navigations, so neither waits for a plugin to be connected. It is never visible to a plugin. `active` is the tab in front - the broker's own show at once,
+  the developer's click within about half a second (the watcher reads `session.panelActiveTab`).
+- `host.browser.get(id or label)`: one browser or tab's brief AS IT IS (nothing navigated or shown), or null - for a
+  plugin that comes back to a tab it opened; `open()` is for opening and navigating.
+

+    /**
+     * THE BROKER'S OWN CONNECTION to the window's Chrome, while it has tabs (the developer, 2026-11-14: a plugin connects
+     * for a call and goes, so most tabs had no title in the tab bar or in /v1/browser). It attaches every tab with a
+     * session of its own, and from it come each tab's title, the developer's navigations, the framing of a page that
+     * refuses it, and which tab is in front - with no plugin connected. A plugin never sees it: it is not a target, and
+     * what it is sent goes nowhere. Its connection ended (the window closed, Chrome's port not there yet): it is started
+     * again, soon after a connection that worked, else less and less often.

+    void watch() {
+      final Client c;
+      synchronized (watchLock) {
+        if (stopped || watcher != null || watchWaiting || tabList().isEmpty()) return;
+        c = new Client(this, null, null, true);
+        watcher = c;
+      }
+      synchronized (clients) { clients.add(c); }
+      Thread t = new Thread(new Runnable() { public void run() {
+        try { c.run(); } finally { synchronized (clients) { clients.remove(c); } }
+        long wait;
+        synchronized (watchLock) {
+          if (watcher == c) watcher = null;
+          if (stopped || tabList().isEmpty()) return;
+          watchWait = c.connected ? 500 : Math.min(10000, Math.max(1000, watchWait * 2));
+          wait = watchWait;
+          watchWaiting = true;
+        }
+        Sandbox.sleep(wait);
+        synchronized (watchLock) { watchWaiting = false; }
+        watch();
+      } }, "cb-panel-broker-watch");
+      t.setDaemon(true);
+      t.start();
+    }
+
+    /** The watcher has its session on this tab: a plugin's connection leaves the tab's title and the developer's navigations to it. */
+    boolean watching(Tab t) {
+      Client w = watcher;
+      TabSession ts = w == null ? null : w.sessions.get(t.id);

+    /** Every open tab's brief, in the order they were opened. */

+    /** The tab with that label (reused: navigated unless url is about:blank, and brought to the front), or a new one. */
+    Map<String, Object> open(String url, String label) {
+      String u = url == null || url.trim().isEmpty() ? "about:blank" : url.trim();
+      String l = label == null || label.trim().isEmpty() ? "default" : label.trim();
+      // (by its LABEL only: a label that is another tab's id would make close() and brief() name two tabs)
+      Tab t = null;
+      synchronized (tabs) {
+        for (Tab x : tabs.values()) if (x.label.equals(l)) { t = x; break; }
+        if (t == null && tabs.containsKey(l)) return no("'" + l + "' is the id of another tab: give this one a label of its own.");
+      }
+      boolean reused = t != null;
+      if (reused) {
+        Map<String, Object> body = Client.obj("tab", t.id);
+        if (!"about:blank".equals(u)) body.put("url", siteOf(u));
+        body.put("show", Boolean.TRUE);
+        tellPage(body);
+      } else {
+        t = openTab(u, l);
+        if (t == null) return no(TOO_MANY);
+      }
+      Map<String, Object> r = brief(t);
+      r.put("reused", Boolean.valueOf(reused));
+      return r;
+    }
+
+    /** Close a tab by its id or label; this panel's own id (or "panel") closes every tab. */
+    Map<String, Object> close(String key) {
+      Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+      m.put("ok", Boolean.TRUE);
+      if (id.equals(key) || "panel".equals(key)) {
+        int n = closeAll();
+        m.put("closed", Boolean.TRUE);
+        m.put("id", id);
+        m.put("tabs", Integer.valueOf(n));
+        return m;
+      }
+      Tab t = tab(key);
+      if (t == null) { m.put("closed", Boolean.FALSE); m.put("id", key); return m; }
+      closeTab(t);
+      m.put("closed", Boolean.TRUE);
+      m.put("id", t.id);
+      m.put("label", t.label);
+      return m;
+    }

+    int closeAll() { List<Tab> all = tabList(); for (Tab t : all) closeTab(t); return all.size(); }
+
+    /** A new tab on u, in front (label null: tab-<n>). null: too many are open. */
+    Tab openTab(String u, String label) {
+      Tab t;
+      synchronized (tabs) {
+        if (tabs.size() >= MAX_TABS) return null;
+        String l = label;
+        if (l == null) { do { l = "tab-" + (++seq); } while (tab(l) != null); }
+        t = new Tab(hex(16), l);
+        tabs.put(t.id, t);
+      }
+      Map<String, Object> body = Client.obj("tab", t.id);
+      body.put("label", t.label);
+      body.put("url", to(u));
+      body.put("show", Boolean.TRUE);
+      tellPage(body);
+      for (Client c : clientList()) c.tabOpened(t);
+      watch();
+      return t;
+    }
+
+    /** The tab is closed: every connection's session on it ends, and its iframe goes. */
+    boolean closeTab(Tab t) {
+      synchronized (tabs) { if (tabs.remove(t.id) == null) return false; }
+      if (t.id.equals(active)) active = "";
+      for (Client c : clientList()) c.tabClosed(t);
+      Client w = watcher;
+      if (w != null && tabList().isEmpty()) w.close();   // (no tab: nothing to watch; the next tab starts it)
+      Map<String, Object> body = Client.obj("tab", t.id);
+      body.put("close", Boolean.TRUE);
+      tellPage(body);
+      return true;
+    }
+
+    /** The tab's url or title changed: every connection that discovers targets hears it (once, however many saw it). */
+    void changed(Tab t) {
+      String k = t.url + "\n" + t.title;
+      synchronized (t) { if (k.equals(t.said) || !isOpen(t)) return; t.said = k; }
+      for (Client c : clientList()) c.tabChanged(t);
+    }
+
+    /** The tab's page has a title: the tab bar shows it. */
+    void titled(Tab t, String title) {
+      if (!isOpen(t)) return;
+      t.title = title;
+      boolean tell;
+      synchronized (t) { tell = !title.equals(t.toldTitle); if (tell) t.toldTitle = title; }
+      if (tell) { Map<String, Object> body = Client.obj("tab", t.id); body.put("title", title); tellPage(body); }
+      changed(t);
+    }

+    /**
+     * Say to code_boss's page what a tab shows - POST /api/panel-browser (PANEL-TABS-SPEC.md): { tab, label, url, show }
+     * puts url in that tab (a new one when it is not open yet), { tab, url, at: true } says the tab is there already
+     * (do not load it), { tab, title }, { tab, show: true }, { tab, close: true }. Nothing more is said of a tab once
+     * it is closed.
+     */
+    void tellPage(Map<String, Object> body) {
+      Tab t = tabById(Json.string(body.get("tab")));
+      if (t == null && !Boolean.TRUE.equals(body.get("close"))) return;
+      if (t != null && body.containsKey("url") && !Boolean.TRUE.equals(body.get("at"))) t.url = Json.string(body.get("url"));
+      if (t != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(body.get("show"))) { active = t.id; shownAt = System.currentTimeMillis(); }
+      synchronized (tellLock) {   // one after another: the page's address line ends on the last thing said
+        HttpURLConnection c = null;
+        try {
+          c = (HttpURLConnection) new URL("http://127.0.0.1:" + inst.port + "/api/panel-browser").openConnection(Proxy.NO_PROXY);
+          c.setConnectTimeout(2000);
+          c.setReadTimeout(5000);
+          c.setRequestMethod("POST");
+          c.setDoOutput(true);
+          c.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json");
+          try (OutputStream o = c.getOutputStream()) { o.write(Json.write(body).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)); }
+          c.getResponseCode();
+        } catch (IOException ignore) {
+        } finally { if (c != null) c.disconnect(); }
+      }

+// a raw DevTools client, as the plugin has one (no Origin, flatten sessions)
+function connect(url, headers = {}) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const u = new URL(url);
+    const s = net.connect(+u.port, u.hostname);
+    s.write(`GET ${u.pathname} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: ${u.host}\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Key: ${Buffer.from(String(Math.random())).toString('base64')}\r\nSec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\n${Object.entries(headers).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}\r\n`).join('')}\r\n`);
+    let buf = Buffer.alloc(0), open = false;
+    const c = { seq: 0, waiting: new Map(), events: [], closed: false };
+    c.send = (method, params = {}, sessionId) => new Promise((r) => { if (c.closed) { r({ error: { message: 'closed' } }); return; } const id = ++c.seq; c.waiting.set(id, r); const p = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params, ...(sessionId ? { sessionId } : {}) })); const mask = Buffer.from([5, 1, 3, 9]); let h; if (p.length < 126) h = Buffer.from([0x81, 0x80 | p.length]); else if (p.length < 65536) { h = Buffer.alloc(4); h[0] = 0x81; h[1] = 0x80 | 126; h.writeUInt16BE(p.length, 2); } else { h = Buffer.alloc(10); h[0] = 0x81; h[1] = 0x80 | 127; h.writeBigUInt64BE(BigInt(p.length), 2); } const q = Buffer.from(p.map((b, i) => b ^ mask[i & 3])); s.write(Buffer.concat([h, mask, q])); });
+    c.ev = async (pred, ms = 6000) => { const t = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t < ms) { const i = c.events.findIndex(pred); if (i >= 0) return c.events.splice(i, 1)[0]; await sleep(40); } return null; };
+    c.close = () => s.destroy();
+    s.on('data', (d) => {
+      buf = Buffer.concat([buf, d]);
+      if (!open) { const i = buf.indexOf('\r\n\r\n'); if (i < 0) return; const head = buf.slice(0, i).toString(); buf = buf.slice(i + 4); if (!/^HTTP\/1\.1 101/.test(head)) { s.destroy(); reject(new Error(head.split('\r\n')[0])); return; } open = true; resolve(c); }
+      for (;;) {
+        if (buf.length < 2) return;
+        let len = buf[1] & 0x7f, off = 2;
+        if (len === 126) { if (buf.length < 4) return; len = buf.readUInt16BE(2); off = 4; } else if (len === 127) { if (buf.length < 10) return; len = Number(buf.readBigUInt64BE(2)); off = 10; }
+        if (buf.length < off + len) return;
+        const m = JSON.parse(buf.slice(off, off + len).toString()); buf = buf.slice(off + len);
+        if (m.id && c.waiting.has(m.id)) { c.waiting.get(m.id)(m); c.waiting.delete(m.id); } else c.events.push(m);
+      }
+    });
+    s.on('error', (e) => { if (!open) reject(e); });
+    s.on('close', () => { c.closed = true; for (const [, r] of c.waiting) r({ error: { message: 'the connection closed' } }); c.waiting.clear(); });
+  });
+}

+/**
+ * TABS IN THE AGENT'S BROWSER - the page and the server (PANEL-TABS-SPEC.md; the developer, 2026-11-14: "If there's only
+ * 1 page open, we don't show a tab bar, if there is we do. The tabs should have titles (to display) and labels or ids").
+ * What a broker tells code_boss (POST /api/panel-browser with a tab), through the real server and page:
+ *   [1] one tab: its iframe, no tab bar
+ *   [2] a second tab: two iframes (iframe[data-cb-tab]), the bar with both, the new one in front, the first behind
+ *   [3] titles: a tab's title is its name in the bar (its label until it has one); a click shows that tab, and the
+ *       server's list says it is the active one
+ *   [4] closed: its iframe goes; one tab left - no bar
+ *   [5] an old broker (no tab in what it says): the tab "default", in #agentFrame, as before

+/**
+ * TABS IN THE AGENT'S BROWSER, without a browser (PANEL-TABS-SPEC.md): the Node broker's tabs as a plugin sees them
+ * (open / list / brief / close, with a stand-in for code_boss's page), and the server's tab list (POST /api/panel-browser)
+ * - volume and edges.
+ *   [1] labels: the default, a new label a new tab, an open label the same tab (reused), every refusal said
+ *   [2] what the page is told: a new tab with its label and url, a reused one shown (and navigated unless about:blank)
+ *   [3] 20 tabs and no more; closing one makes room; list() in the order opened; ids 32-hex and unique
+ *   [4] brief(key) and close(key): by id, by label, unknown, "panel" (every tab), then a tab again
+ *   [5] a label that is another tab's id is refused (close and brief would name two tabs)
+ *   [6] the server: tabs added, titled, closed; the active one; an old broker's body; refusals; 60 tabs, 200 titles
+ * Run: node test/test-panel-tabs-unit.mjs
+ */

+  const bad = [];

=== add test/test-send-pinned-e2e.mjs
new fd2986db02c86840637f6361
+/**
+ * PINNED WHILE YOU TYPE AND WHEN YOU SEND (the developer, 2026-11-14: "when I'm at the bottom of the chat and submit a
+ * message, I only see half of it appear. It's like it doesn't keep me pinned to the bottom so I see my new message").
+ * The box grows as a long message is typed (its height set to auto first, which pulls the log's scrollTop up for an
+ * instant); the scroll event read a frame later saw the box's new height as a gap and let go of the bottom.
+ *   [1] typing a 12-line message at the bottom: the chat stays at the bottom, pinned
+ *   [2] sent: the whole message is in view, and stays in view until the reply comes
+ * Run: node test/test-send-pinned-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sendpin-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const PORT = 29657;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => p && existsSync(p));
+const LONG = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => 'Line ' + i + ' of a long answer, long enough to fill the view.').join('\n\n');
+const SLOW = 3000;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { await sleep(SLOW); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: LONG } }] }; },
+  async complete() { await sleep(SLOW); return { choices: [{ message: { content: LONG } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sendpin-proj-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), 'x\n');
+const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+const page = await browser.newPage();
+await page.setViewport({ width: 1400, height: 900 });
+await page.goto(`http://localhost:${PORT}/`);
+await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+await sleep(800);
+const idle = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 150; i++) { const s = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state?limit=1`)).json(); if (s.status !== 'running') return; await sleep(200); } };
+const send = async (text) => { await page.click('#input'); await page.keyboard.type(text.replace(/\n/g, '')); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); };
+for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { await send('fill ' + i); await sleep(400); await idle(); await sleep(300); }
+const where = () => page.evaluate(() => {
+  const users = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .row.user, #log .user')];
+  const u = users[users.length - 1];
+  const sc = document.querySelector('#log')?.closest('.log-scroll') || document.querySelector('#logScroll') || document.querySelector('#log').parentElement;
+  if (!u || !sc) return { none: true };
+  const r = u.getBoundingClientRect(), s = sc.getBoundingClientRect();
+  const box = document.querySelector('#input')?.closest('.composer, .input-area, form') || document.querySelector('#input');
+  const b = box ? box.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
+  return { text: (u.textContent || '').slice(0, 30), userTop: Math.round(r.top), userBottom: Math.round(r.bottom), scTop: Math.round(s.top), scBottom: Math.round(s.bottom), boxTop: b ? Math.round(b.top) : null, gap: Math.round(sc.scrollHeight - sc.scrollTop - sc.clientHeight), status: window.session && window.session.status };
+});
+
+// a message of several lines (shift+enter), as the developer types one
+await page.click('#input');
+const NL = 12;
+for (let i = 0; i < NL; i++) { await page.keyboard.type('the new message, line ' + i); if (i < NL - 1) { await page.keyboard.down('Shift'); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); await page.keyboard.up('Shift'); } }
+await sleep(200);
+const typed = await where(), stuck = await page.evaluate(() => _stickBottom);
+console.log('\n[1] typing a long message at the bottom');
+ok(stuck === true && typed.gap <= 2, `still at the bottom and pinned as the box grew (gap ${typed.gap}, pinned ${stuck}, box top ${typed.boxTop})`);
+await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+const t0 = Date.now();
+console.log('\n[2] sent: the whole message in view');
+let worst = null;
+for (let i = 0; i < 40 && Date.now() - t0 < 2400; i++) { await sleep(i < 20 ? 16 : 100); const w = await where(); if (/the new message/.test(w.text || '') && (w.userTop < w.scTop || w.userBottom > w.scBottom)) { worst = w; break; } }
+const end = await where();
+ok(!worst && /the new message/.test(end.text || '') && end.userTop >= end.scTop && end.userBottom <= end.scBottom, `the message stays whole in view until the reply (${JSON.stringify(worst || end)})`);
+await browser.close(); server.close();
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
